spec	E33-D04 — Design-system contributions and motion spec for the emulated-algo surfaces › 7. The motion spec › 7.1 Cadence and "no implied sub-second precision" (RULED BQ-10)#1	s4	1.

The motion spec › 7.1 Cadence and "no implied sub-second precision" (RULED BQ-10)#1	s5	**No transition is longer than the sample interval** it animates between (a move ends before the next sample can arrive).
spec	E33-D04 — Design-system contributions and motion spec for the emulated-algo surfaces › 7. The motion spec › 7.1 Cadence and "no implied sub-second precision" (RULED BQ-10)#1	s6	2.

spec	E33-D04 — Design-system contributions and motion spec for the emulated-algo surfaces › 7. The motion spec › 7.1 Cadence and "no implied sub-second precision" (RULED BQ-10)#1	s8	3.
spec	E33-D04 — Design-system contributions and motion spec for the emulated-algo surfaces › 7. The motion spec › 7.1 Cadence and "no implied sub-second precision" (RULED BQ-10)#1	s9	**No continuous motion between samples** — once a value has rendered, nothing loops, pulses or drifts while waiting for the next sample.
spec	E33-D04 — Design-system contributions and motion spec for the emulated-algo surfaces › 7. spec	E33-D04 — Design-system contributions and motion spec for the emulated-algo surfaces › 8. Token-drift audit (RULED BQ-16, BQ-17)#1	heading	## 8. Token-drift audit (RULED BQ-16, BQ-17)
spec	E33-D04 — Design-system contributions and motion spec for the emulated-algo surfaces › 8. Token-drift audit (RULED BQ-16, BQ-17)#1	s1	**Scope.** The token bindings of D02 (SCR-066 / 067 / 068), D03 (the SCR-069 OCO half and SCR-070) and H03, at their merged text; the spacing and size names they use; their contrast tables (D02's and D03's gated pairs).
spec	E33-D04 — Design-system contributions and motion spec for the emulated-algo surfaces › 8. Token-drift audit (RULED BQ-16, BQ-17)#1	s2	**Method.** The tool reads each file with `git show 965cf14090:<path>`, extracts every backticked name matching `color.|space.|size.|type.|radius.|elevation.|opacity.|motion.`, resolves each in dark, light and high contrast, and re-measures every pair.
spec	E33-D04 — Design-system contributions and motion spec for the emulated-algo surfaces › 8. Token-drift audit (RULED BQ-16, BQ-17)#1	s3	<!--=COUNT:tokens.bound--> names are bound; <!--=COUNT:drift.class-a--> resolve as shipped semantic names (or primitives no semantic name aliases), and the rest are class (b) or (d); class (c) is a binding, not a name, found by reading each binding against the rulings, so it lives in the ledger only.
spec	E33-D04 — Design-system contributions and motion spec for the emulated-algo surfaces › 8. Token-drift audit (RULED BQ-16, BQ-17)#1	s4	**Classes:** (a) shipped and consistent; (b) missing; (c) shipped but divergent from a design-system-owner ruling; (d) a primitive where a semantic name exists.
spec	E33-D04 — Design-system contributions and motion spec for the emulated-algo surfaces › 8. Token-drift audit (RULED BQ-16, BQ-17)#1	s5	Spacing and sizes are checked against `16`'s density and size scales.
spec	E33-D04 — Design-system contributions and motion spec for the emulated-algo surfaces › 8. Token-drift audit (RULED BQ-16, BQ-17)#1	s6	No pixel audit of the merged boards: they were bound to these text tables and pinned by their boards PRs.
spec	E33-D04 — Design-system contributions and motion spec for the emulated-algo surfaces › 8. Token-drift audit (RULED BQ-16, BQ-17)#1	s7	**Result.** <!--=COUNT:drift.rows--> ledger rows (hand-off §E): <!--=COUNT:drift.corrected--> corrected by an A-row to the frame's owner with an exact replacement, <!--=COUNT:drift.justified--> justified by a "Deliberate exception (DR-n):" line in the copy-ready entry or a cite the row confirms, <!--=COUNT:drift.unresolved--> unresolved.
spec	E33-D04 — Design-system contributions and motion spec for the emulated-algo surfaces › 8. Token-drift audit (RULED BQ-16, BQ-17)#1	s8	Every name outside class (a) must have a ledger row, or `--check` fails.
